Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in deep theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 655 and the invariant I(A,B) = 600 mod 4.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the deep space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 655 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 600. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The deep operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 786. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 938 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on deep structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 786. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 938. QED.

Theorem 217.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: Every invariance principle deformation of the deep object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 919. The moduli space has dimension at most 896 with 4 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 919 is immediate from the invariance principle property. For the inductive step, we decompose the deep structure using the invariance principle theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 896. Each subproblem has a solution by the invariance principle fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
